# Commands · H · part 2

1,350 entries, harden to health-check. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **harden**: Promote the current branch to a stricter lane (Sprint to Standard, or Standard to Certified) when a spike turned out to matter. Recovers the spec from the diff and names what the higher bar requires; nothing is regenerated. ([Expeed-Software/exloom](https://github.com/Expeed-Software/exloom/blob/HEAD/plugins/exloom/commands/harden.md))
- **harden**: Turns your product's security controls on, one at a time, in an order that cannot lock out customers already running your software. Each control has something your client must do first, and this checks your code does it before it tells you to flip anything. Run this after your integration ships and works. ([ForeigndevX/licentry-integrate](https://github.com/ForeigndevX/licentry-integrate/blob/HEAD/commands/harden.md))
- **harden**: Pressure-test a plan, decision, or approach before committing to it, surfacing failure modes and unstated assumptions. ([GundysShrubberies/claude-plugins](https://github.com/GundysShrubberies/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/gundy-kit/commands/harden.md))
- **harden**: Close the gaps a fast build leaves behind — missing tests on the demo path, unhandled errors, absent loading and empty states, secret hygiene, and env var checks. ([jeevanparajuli856/OncoReady](https://github.com/jeevanparajuli856/OncoReady/blob/HEAD/commands/harden.md))

- **harden-api**: Run an OWASP API Security Top 10 (2023) pass over an API or endpoint — object-level (BOLA) and function-level (BFLA) authorization, token/scope validation, property-level authorization (BOPLA), resource-consumption limits, SSRF, misconfiguration, inventory, and unsafe upstream consumption. Designs controls; routes the verdict to security-reviewer. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/api-engineering/commands/harden-api.md) · ★9)
- **harden-artifact**: Make the build artifact trustworthy: reproducible, minimal, non-root, with an SBOM and SLSA provenance. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/devops-cicd/commands/harden-artifact.md) · ★9)
- **harden-auth**: Deployment hardening step 2 — review auth/session/authz surfaces. Identifies insecure cookies, broad CORS, missing CSRF, unauthenticated state-changing routes, weak password storage, JWT secret handling. Read-only by default; --fix tightens cookie flags and obvious CORS misconfigs only with confirmation. Fire on `/harden-auth`. ([jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev](https://github.com/jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-auth.md))
- **harden-config**: Deployment hardening step 5 — validate runtime/deploy config per target. Railway: railway.json/toml, healthcheck, restart policy. npm: package.json publish metadata. Public git: README + LICENSE present, no internal hostnames. Read-only; --fix applies obvious additions (license field, engines, healthcheck path) with confirmation. Fire on `/harden-config`. ([jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev](https://github.com/jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-config.md))
- **harden-container**: Produce a small, non-root, distroless, digest-pinned container with dropped caps and read-only root FS. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/cloud-native-kubernetes/commands/harden-container.md) · ★9)

- **harden-for-deploy**: Run the full deployment-hardening series (secrets → auth → deps → licenses → config → lint → tests → observability → review) in order. Fire on `/harden-for-deploy` or "is this safe to deploy?" ([jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev](https://github.com/jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-for-deploy.md))

- **harden-instructions**: Harden any instruction file — CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, a skill, agent, command or rule, here or in another repo — so each rule lives where the model cannot lose it and what stays in prose is phrased to be recalled. ([pmvanev/phil-claude-plugin](https://github.com/pmvanev/phil-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-instructions.md))
- **harden-licenses**: Deployment hardening step 4 — resolve repo license, enumerate every dep's license (direct + transitive), flag conflicts (GPL/AGPL pulled into MIT/Apache, UNKNOWN, custom non-OSI). Distinguishes runtime vs dev deps. Stricter rules for npm publish + public git. Read-only — never auto-removes deps. Fire on `/harden-licenses`. ([jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev](https://github.com/jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-licenses.md))
- **harden-lint**: Deployment hardening step 6 — static analysis gate. Verify a linter / type-checker / formatter is configured, run them, and flag errors, security-rule hits, suppression drift, and unenforced gates. Read-only; --fix applies ONLY the linter's own safe autofixes, with confirmation. Never silences a rule to make lint pass. Fire on `/harden-lint`. ([jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev](https://github.com/jchigg2000-git/claude-code-dev/blob/HEAD/commands/harden-lint.md))

- **hatch.ar**: كيف يعمل أمر ⁨HATCH⁩ في أوتوكاد، مطالبةً بعد مطالبة: نقطة داخلية أو كائنات، والنقش ومقياسه وزاويته، والجزر. جرّبه مجانًا في متصفحك. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ar.md))
- **hatch.cs**: Jak v AutoCADu šrafovat příkazem ŠRAFY (HATCH), výzvu po výzvě: bod uvnitř nebo hraniční objekty, vzor, měřítko, úhel, ostrovy. Vyzkoušejte zdarma online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.cs.md))
- **hatch.de**: Wie SCHRAFF (HATCH, Alias SCH, GS) in AutoCAD eine Fläche schraffiert, Schritt für Schritt: Punkt oder Objekte, Muster, Maßstab, Winkel, Inseln. Zum Testen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.de.md))
- **hatch.es**: Cómo funciona SOMBREA (HATCH, alias SB) de AutoCAD en español: punto interno u objetos, patrón, escala, ángulo e islas. Pruébalo gratis online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.es.md))
- **hatch.fr**: Hachures dans AutoCAD en français avec HACHURES (HATCH, alias H), invite par invite : point interne ou objets, motif, échelle, angle, îlots. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.fr.md))
- **hatch.hu**: Sraffozás az AutoCAD SRAFFOZ (HATCH, SF) parancsával: belső pont vagy határoló objektumok, minta, lépték, szög, szigetek. Próbálja ki ingyen, online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.hu.md))
- **hatch.it**: Il tratteggio in AutoCAD in italiano con TRATTEGGIO (HATCH, alias PTR, RE), richiesta per richiesta: punto interno o oggetti, modello, scala, angolo, isole. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.it.md))
- **hatch.ja**: AutoCAD のハッチング(HATCH、H)で領域を埋める方法をプロンプトの順に説明します。内側の点またはオブジェクト、パターン、尺度と角度、島。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ja.md))
- **hatch.ko**: AutoCAD 해치(HATCH, H), 캐드 해치를 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 내부 점 또는 객체 선택, 해치 패턴, 축척과 각도, 고립영역. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ko.md))
- **hatch.pl**: Jak kreskować w AutoCADzie poleceniem KRESKUJ (HATCH), monit po monicie: punkt wewnątrz lub obiekty obwiedni, wzór, skala, kąt, wyspy. Wypróbuj za darmo online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.pl.md))
- **hatch.pt-BR**: Como funciona o HATCH do AutoCAD em português, HACHURA (alias HC): ponto interno ou objetos, padrão, escala, ângulo e ilhas. Experimente grátis online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.pt-BR.md))
- **hatch.ru**: Как штриховать в AutoCAD командой ШТРИХ (HATCH), запрос за запросом: точка внутри или объекты контура, образец, масштаб, угол, островки. Попробуйте бесплатно. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.ru.md))
- **hatch.zh-CN**: CAD 图案填充怎么操作：按提示顺序讲解 AutoCAD 的 HATCH（快捷键 H）：拾取内部点或选择对象、图案、比例和角度，以及孤岛。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/hatch.zh-CN.md))
